\displaystyle\;\lim\,\Big\|\Big(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\lambda}{2m}$}}\Sigma^{\dagger}+\tilde{\Sigma}_{\sigma,\tau}\Big)^{1/2}\Sigma^{1/2}P_{\Sigma_{\rm new}}(\Sigma^{\dagger})^{1/2}\Big(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{2\sigma_2 \tau_2 \lambda}{2m}$}}\Sigma^{\dagger}+I_{p}\Big)^{\dagger}P_{*}\Big\|^{2}\;.

Combining the calculations above, we obtain that

\displaystyle\tilde{M}_{\mathbf{g},\sigma,\tau,\theta}\;\overset{\mathbb{P}}{\rightarrow}

\displaystyle\;-\bar{\chi}^{r,\theta,\sigma,\tau}_{1}+\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\bar\chi^{r, \theta,\sigma, \tau}_3}{\bar\chi^{r, \theta,\sigma, \tau}_2}$}}\,\times\,\min_{\mu\in P_{\Sigma_{\rm new}}(S)}\Big(c(\mu)\sqrt{\bar{\chi}^{r,\theta,\sigma,\tau}_{2}}-\sqrt{\bar{\chi}^{r,\theta,\sigma,\tau}_{2}}\Big)^{2}\;.

Notice that
\|\Sigma^{1/2}_{\rm new}\mu\|=c(\mu)\|\mathbf{g}^{\prime}\|\xrightarrow{\mathbb{P}}c(\mu)\sqrt{\bar{\chi}^{r,\theta,\sigma,\tau}_{2}}
, and recall that our two choices of
S
only differs through
\|\Sigma^{1/2}_{\rm new}\mu\|-(\bar{\chi}^{\bar{r},\bar{\theta},\bar{\sigma},\bar{\tau}}_{2})^{1/2}
, where
\bar{r}=(\bar{r}_{1},\bar{r}_{2})
,
\bar{\sigma}=(\bar{\sigma}_{1},\bar{\sigma}_{2})
,
\bar{\theta}
and
\bar{\tau}=(\bar{\tau}_{1},\bar{\tau}_{2})
are the optimal solutions to (
DO
). This implies that

\displaystyle\tilde{M}_{\mathbf{g},\sigma,\tau,\theta}\;\overset{\mathbb{P}}{\rightarrow}

\displaystyle\;-\bar{\chi}^{r,\theta,\sigma,\tau}_{1}+\epsilon_{S}^{2}\,\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\bar\chi^{r,\theta, \sigma, \tau}_3}{\bar\chi^{r, \theta, \sigma, \tau}_2}$}}\;,

where
\epsilon_{S}=0
for
S=\mathcal{S}_{p}
and
\epsilon_{S}=\epsilon
for
S=\mathcal{S}_{\epsilon}^{c}
. Substituting this back into the overall optimization, we can approximate (
SO
) in distribution by

\displaystyle\min_{\begin{subarray}{c}\alpha\in S^{\alpha}\\
(\sigma_{1},\sigma_{2})\in S_{\sigma_{1}}\times S_{\sigma_{2}}\\
\nu_{1},\nu_{2}\geq 0\end{subarray}}\,\max_{\begin{subarray}{c}(r_{1},r_{2})\in S_{r_{1}}\times S_{r_{2}}\\
\tau_{1},\tau_{2}\geq 0\\
\theta\in\mathbb{R}\end{subarray}}\,

\displaystyle\,-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\sigma_1}{2 \tau_1}$}}-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\sigma_2}{2 \tau_2}$}}+\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{r_1}{2 \nu_1}$}}+\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{r_2}{2 \nu_2}$}}+\alpha\theta\bar{\kappa}_{*}^{2}-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\alpha^2 \bar\kappa_*^2}{2 \sigma_2 \tau_2}$}}-\bar{\chi}^{r,\theta,\sigma,\tau}_{1}+\epsilon_{S}^{2}\,\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\bar\chi^{r, \theta, \sigma, \tau}_3}{\bar\chi^{r, \theta, \sigma, \tau}_2}$}}

\displaystyle\,+\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1}{mk}$}}M_{\mathbf{y},\tilde{\mathbf{h}}_{\alpha,\sigma},r,\nu}-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1}{2 r_2 \nu_2 mk }$}}\|\mathbf{y}\|^{2}-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1}{mk}$}}\mathbf{y}^{\intercal}\tilde{\mathbf{h}}_{\alpha,\sigma}\;.

Compute terms involving
\mathbf{y}
and
\tilde{\mathbf{h}}_{\alpha,\sigma}
.
Recall that
\tilde{\mathbf{h}}_{\alpha,\sigma}=\kappa_{*}\alpha\mathbf{q}-\sigma_{1}\mathbf{h}_{1}-\frac{\sigma_{2}}{\sqrt{k}}J_{mk}\mathbf{h}_{2}
, where
\mathbf{q}=\mathbf{q}(\mathbf{G}^{\Phi})=\frac{1}{\kappa_{*}\sqrt{p}}\mathbf{G}^{\Phi}v(\beta^{*})
, and
\mathbf{h}_{1}
and
\mathbf{h}_{2}
are i.i.d. standard
\mathbb{R}^{mk}
Gaussians independent of
\mathbf{q}(\mathbf{G}^{\Phi})
and
\mathbf{y}=\mathbf{y}(\mathbf{G})
. Also recall that
\mathbf{y}=\mathbf{y}P_{mk}=\frac{1}{k}\mathbf{y}J_{mk}
. We can then express the last two terms of the loss above as

\displaystyle-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1}{2 r_2 \nu_2 mk }$}}\|\mathbf{y}\|^{2}-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1}{mk}$}}\mathbf{y}^{\intercal}\tilde{\mathbf{h}}_{\alpha,\sigma}\;=

\displaystyle\;-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1}{2 r_2 \nu_2 mk }$}}\|\mathbf{y}\|^{2}-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\kappa_* \alpha}{mk}$}}\mathbf{y}^{\intercal}\mathbf{q}+\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\sigma_1}{mk}$}}\mathbf{y}^{\intercal}\mathbf{h}_{1}+\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\sigma_2}{m\sqrt{k}}$}}\mathbf{y}^{\intercal}P_{mk}\mathbf{h}_{2}\;.

(129)

Since
\mathbf{h}_{1}
and
\mathbf{h}_{2}
are zero-mean and
\mathbf{y}
is coordinate-wise bounded by one, by the weak law of large numbers,

\displaystyle\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1}{mk}$}}\,\mathbf{y}^{\intercal}\mathbf{h}_{1}\;\xrightarrow{\mathbb{P}}

\displaystyle\;0

and

\displaystyle\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1}{m\sqrt{k}}$}}\,\mathbf{y}^{\intercal}P_{mk}\mathbf{h}_{2}\;\xrightarrow{\mathbb{P}}

\displaystyle\;0\;.

(130)

To handle the first two terms, recall that

\displaystyle\mathbf{y}\;=\;\big(\,\underbrace{y_{1}(\Sigma_{o}^{1/2}G_{1}),\ldots,y_{1}(\Sigma_{o}^{1/2}G_{1})}_{\textrm{repeated $k$ times}},\;\ldots,\;\underbrace{y_{n}(\Sigma_{o}^{1/2}G_{n}),\ldots,y_{n}(\Sigma_{o}^{1/2}G_{n})}_{\textrm{repeated $k$ times}}\,\big)^{\intercal}\;,

where
y_{i}(\Sigma_{o}^{1/2}G_{i})
’s are i.i.d. by definition. Therefore by the weak law of large numbers,

\displaystyle\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1}{mk}$}}\|\mathbf{y}\|^{2}\;=\;\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1}{m}$}}\msum_{i=1}^{m}y_{i}(\Sigma_{o}^{1/2}G_{i})\;\xrightarrow{\mathbb{P}}

\displaystyle\;\mathbb{E}[y_{1}(\Sigma_{o}^{1/2}G_{1})]\;=\;\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1}{2}$}}\;.

(131)

In the last equality, we recall that

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\big(y_{1}(\Sigma_{o}^{1/2}G_{1})=1\,\big|\,G_{1}\big)\;=\;\sigma(G_{1}^{\intercal}\Sigma_{o}^{1/2}\beta^{*})\;.

y_{i}\in\{0,1\}
is a logistic variable evaluated at a random input
(\Sigma_{o}^{1/2}G_{1})^{\intercal}\beta^{*}
that is symmetric about zero. On the other hand, recalling that
v(\beta^{*})=\sqrt{p}\Sigma_{*}\Sigma_{o}^{1/2}\beta^{*}
,

\displaystyle\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1}{mk}$}}\mathbf{y}^{\intercal}\mathbf{q}\;=

\displaystyle\;\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1}{mk}$}}\msum_{i\leq m}\msum_{j\leq k}y_{i}(\Sigma_{o}^{1/2}G_{i})\,\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1}{\kappa_* \sqrt{p}}$}}\big(G^{\Phi}_{ij}\big)^{\intercal}v(\beta^{*})

\displaystyle\;=

\displaystyle\;\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1}{\kappa_*}$}}\,\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1}{mk}$}}\msum_{i\leq m}\msum_{j\leq k}y_{i}(\Sigma_{o}^{1/2}G_{i})\,\big(G^{\Phi}_{ij}\big)^{\intercal}\Sigma_{*}\Sigma_{o}^{1/2}\beta^{*}\;.

(132)

Notice that each
y_{i}(\Sigma_{o}^{1/2}G_{i})
depends on
G_{i}
only through
G_{i}^{\intercal}\Sigma_{o}^{1/2}\beta^{*}
, so
G_{i}
and
G^{\Phi}_{ij}
appear in each summand only via the Gaussian vector

\displaystyle\begin{pmatrix}G_{i}^{\intercal}\Sigma_{o}^{1/2}\beta^{*}\\
\big(G^{\Phi}_{i1}\big)^{\intercal}\Sigma_{*}\Sigma_{o}^{1/2}\beta^{*}\\
\vdots\\
\big(G^{\Phi}_{ik}\big)^{\intercal}\Sigma_{*}\Sigma_{o}^{1/2}\beta^{*}\end{pmatrix}\;\sim

\displaystyle\;\mathcal{N}\bigg(0\,,\,\begin{pmatrix}(\beta^{*})^{\intercal}\Sigma_{o}\beta^{*}&(\beta^{*})^{\intercal}\Sigma_{o}^{1/2}\Sigma_{*}\Sigma_{o}^{1/2}\beta^{*}&\ldots&\\
(\beta^{*})^{\intercal}\Sigma_{o}^{1/2}\Sigma_{*}\Sigma_{o}^{1/2}\beta^{*}&(\beta^{*})^{\intercal}\Sigma_{o}^{1/2}\Sigma_{*}\Sigma_{o}^{1/2}\beta^{*}&&\\
\vdots&&\ddots&\\
\end{pmatrix}\bigg)\overset{d}{\rightarrow}\begin{pmatrix}\bar{\kappa}_{o}\bar{Z}_{0}+\bar{\kappa}_{*}\bar{Z}_{1}\\
\bar{\kappa}_{*}\bar{Z}_{1}\\
\vdots\\
\bar{\kappa}_{*}\bar{Z}_{1}\end{pmatrix}\;,

where we recall that by
Assumption

11
,

\displaystyle\Sigma_{o}^{1/2}\,\text{\rm Cov}[G_{i},G^{\Phi}_{i1}]\,\Sigma_{*}\Sigma_{o}^{1/2}\;=

\displaystyle\;\Sigma_{o}^{1/2}(\Sigma_{*})^{2}\Sigma_{o}^{1/2}\;=\;\Sigma_{o}^{1/2}\Sigma_{*}\Sigma_{o}^{1/2}\;,

\displaystyle\Sigma_{o}^{1/2}\,\Sigma_{*}\text{\rm Cov}[G^{\Phi}_{i1},G^{\Phi}_{i2}]\,\Sigma_{*}\Sigma_{o}^{1/2}\;=

\displaystyle\;\Sigma_{o}^{1/2}(\Sigma_{*})^{2}\Sigma_{o}^{1/2}\;=\;\Sigma_{o}^{1/2}\Sigma_{*}\Sigma_{o}^{1/2}\;,

\bar{Z}_{0}
and
\bar{Z}_{1}
are two i.i.d. standard normals and

\displaystyle\bar{\kappa}_{*}\;\coloneqq\;\lim_{p\rightarrow\infty}\kappa_{*}\;=\;\lim_{p\rightarrow\infty}\|\Sigma_{*}\Sigma_{o}^{1/2}\beta^{*}\|\;,\qquad\bar{\kappa}_{o}\;\coloneqq\;\lim_{p\rightarrow\infty}\|(I_{p}-\Sigma_{*})\Sigma_{o}^{1/2}\beta^{*}\|\;.

Then by the law of large numbers, we have

\displaystyle\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\kappa_*}{mk}$}}\,\mathbf{y}^{\intercal}\mathbf{q}\;\xrightarrow{\mathbb{P}}\;\,\mathbb{E}\big[\sigma(\bar{\kappa}_{o}\bar{Z}_{0}+\bar{\kappa}_{*}\bar{Z}_{1})\bar{\kappa}_{*}\bar{Z}_{1}\big]\;.

(133)

Combining (
129
), (
130
), (
131
) and (
133
) gives

\displaystyle-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1}{2 r_2 \nu_2 mk }$}}\|\mathbf{y}\|^{2}-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1}{mk}$}}\mathbf{y}^{\intercal}\tilde{\mathbf{h}}_{\alpha,\sigma}\;\xrightarrow{\mathbb{P}}\;-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1}{4 r_2 \nu_2}$}}-\alpha\,\mathbb{E}\big[\sigma(\bar{\kappa}_{o}\bar{Z}_{0}+\bar{\kappa}_{*}\bar{Z}_{1})\bar{\kappa}_{*}\bar{Z}_{1}\big]\;,

so the optimization can be approximated by

\displaystyle\min_{\begin{subarray}{c}\alpha\in S^{\alpha}\\
(\sigma_{1},\sigma_{2})\in S_{\sigma_{1}}\times S_{\sigma_{2}}\\
\nu_{1},\nu_{2}\geq 0\end{subarray}}\,\max_{\begin{subarray}{c}(r_{1},r_{2})\in S_{r_{1}}\times S_{r_{2}}\\
\tau_{1},\tau_{2}\geq 0\\
\theta\in\mathbb{R}\end{subarray}}\,

\displaystyle\,-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\sigma_1}{2 \tau_1}$}}-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\sigma_2}{2 \tau_2}$}}+\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{r_1}{2 \nu_1}$}}+\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{r_2}{2 \nu_2}$}}+\alpha\theta\bar{\kappa}_{*}^{2}-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\alpha^2 \bar\kappa_*^2}{2 \sigma_2 \tau_2}$}}-\bar{\chi}^{r,\theta,\sigma,\tau}_{1}+\epsilon_{S}^{2}\,\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\bar\chi^{r, \theta, \sigma, \tau}_3}{\bar\chi^{r, \theta, \sigma, \tau}_2}$}}-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1}{4 r_2 \nu_2}$}}

\displaystyle\,-\alpha\,\mathbb{E}\big[\sigma(\bar{\kappa}_{o}\bar{Z}_{0}+\bar{\kappa}_{*}\bar{Z}_{1})\bar{\kappa}_{*}\bar{Z}_{1}\big]+\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1}{mk}$}}M_{\mathbf{y},\tilde{\mathbf{h}}_{\alpha,\sigma},r,\nu}\;.

(134)

Computing the nested Moreau envelope.
We are left with

\displaystyle\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1}{mk}$}}M_{\mathbf{y},\tilde{\mathbf{h}}_{\alpha,\sigma},r,\nu}\;=

\displaystyle\;\min_{u_{2}\in P_{mk}(S_{u})}\,\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1}{mk}$}}M^{\perp}_{\tilde{\mathbf{h}}_{\alpha,\sigma},r,\nu}(u_{2})+\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{r_2 \nu_2}{2 mk}$}}\Big\|P_{mk}\big(u_{2}-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1}{r_2 \nu_2}$}}\mathbf{y}-\tilde{\mathbf{h}}_{\alpha,\sigma}\big)\Big\|^{2}

\displaystyle\;=

\displaystyle\;\min_{u_{2}\in P_{mk}(S_{u})}\,\min_{u_{1}\in P_{mk}^{\perp}(S_{u})}\,\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1}{mk}$}}\mathbf{1}_{mk}^{\intercal}\,\rho(u_{1}+u_{2})+\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{r_2 \nu_2}{2 mk}$}}\Big\|P_{mk}\big(u_{2}-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1}{r_2 \nu_2}$}}\mathbf{y}-\tilde{\mathbf{h}}_{\alpha,\sigma}\big)\Big\|^{2}

\displaystyle\;\hskip 90.00014pt+\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{r_1 \nu_1}{ 2 mk}$}}\big\|P^{\perp}_{mk}(u_{1}-\tilde{\mathbf{h}}_{\alpha,\sigma})\big\|^{2}\;.

Write
u_{1ij}
as the
(i,j)
-th coordinate of
u_{1}\in\mathbb{R}^{mk}
and similarly write
u_{2ij}
for that of
u_{2}
,
q_{ij}
for
\mathbf{q}
,
h_{1ij}
for
\mathbf{h}_{1}
and
h_{2ij}
for
\mathbf{h}_{2}
. Recalling the definition of
\rho
,
P_{mk}=\frac{1}{k}J_{mk}
and
P_{mk}^{\perp}=I_{mk}-P_{mk}
, we can re-express the loss above as

\displaystyle\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1}{mk}$}}\msum_{i,j=1}^{k}L_{ij}(u_{1},u_{2})\;,

where

\displaystyle L_{ij}(u_{1},u_{2})\,

\displaystyle\;\coloneqq\;\log( 1 + e^{u_{1ij} + u_{2ij}} )

\displaystyle\;+\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{r_2 \nu_2}{2}$}}\Big(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1}{k}$}}\msum_{j^{\prime}=1}^{k}\big(u_{2ij^{\prime}}-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1}{r_2 \nu_2}$}}y_{i}(\Sigma_{o}^{1/2}G_{i})-\kappa_{*}\alpha q_{ij^{\prime}}+\sigma_{1}h_{1ij^{\prime}}+\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\sigma_2}{\sqrt{k}}$}}\big(\msum_{j^{\prime\prime}\leq k}h_{2ij^{\prime\prime}}\big)\big)\Big)^{2}

\displaystyle\;+\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{r_1 \nu_1}{ 2}$}}(u_{1ij}-\kappa_{*}\alpha q_{ij}+\sigma_{1}h_{1ij})^{2}-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{r_1 \nu_1}{ 2}$}}\Big(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1}{k}$}}\msum_{j^{\prime}=1}^{k}(u_{1ij^{\prime}}-\kappa_{*}\alpha q_{ij^{\prime}}+\sigma_{1}h_{1ij^{\prime}})\Big)^{2}\;.

Consider the
\mathbb{R}^{k}
-valued vectors
u_{1i}=(u_{1i1},\ldots,u_{1ik})
and
u_{2i}=(u_{2i1},\ldots,u_{2ik})
for
1\leq i\leq m
. Notice that the loss
L_{ij}(u_{1},u_{2})=\tilde{L}_{ij}(u_{1i},u_{2i})
only depends on
u_{1}
and
u_{2}
through
u_{1i},u_{2i}
. This allows us to rewrite

\displaystyle\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1}{mk}$}}M_{\mathbf{y},\tilde{\mathbf{h}}_{\alpha,\sigma},r,\nu}\;=\;\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1}{m}$}}\msum_{i=1}^{m}\min_{u_{2i}\in(P_{mk}(S_{u}))_{i}}\,\min_{u_{1i}\in(P_{mk}^{\perp}(S_{u}))_{i}}\,\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1}{k}$}}\msum_{j=1}^{k}\tilde{L}_{ij}(u_{1i},u_{2i})\;,

where
(P_{mk}(S_{u}))_{i}
and
(P_{mk}^{\perp}(S_{u}))_{i}
are the corresponding subspaces in which
u_{2i}
and
u_{1i}
take values. Since
S_{u}
is closed under permutation of its
m
blocks of
k
coordinates, the
m
summands above are i.i.d., which allows us to apply a weak law of large numbers to the above average. Also note that the minima are over
\mathbb{R}^{k}
-valued vectors, which allows us again to take a limit with
p\rightarrow\infty
inside the loss function. Using the computation of
y_{ij}q_{ij}
via
\bar{Z}_{0}
and
\bar{Z}_{1}
in (
132
), we obtain that
M_{\mathbf{y},\tilde{\mathbf{h}}_{\alpha,\sigma},r,\nu}
can be approximated by

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\bigg[

\displaystyle\min_{\begin{subarray}{c}u^{\prime}\in(P_{mk}(S_{u}))_{1}\\
u^{\prime\prime}\in(P_{mk}^{\perp}(S_{u}))_{1}\end{subarray}}\;\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1}{k}$}}\msum_{j=1}^{k}\log(1+e^{u'_j+u''_j})

\displaystyle\;\qquad+\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{r_2 \nu_2}{2}$}}\Big(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1}{k}$}}\msum_{j=1}^{k}\big(u^{\prime}_{j}-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1}{r_2 \nu_2}$}}\mathbb{I}_{\geq 0}\{\bar{\kappa}_{o}\bar{Z}_{0}+\bar{\kappa}_{*}\bar{Z}_{1}-\varepsilon_{1}\}-\alpha\bar{\kappa}_{*}\bar{Z}_{1}+\sigma_{1}\eta_{j}+\sigma_{2}\bar{Z}_{2}\big)\Big)^{2}

\displaystyle\;\qquad+\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{r_1 \nu_1}{2}$}}\,\Big(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1}{k}$}}\msum_{j=1}^{k}(u^{\prime\prime}_{j}-\alpha\bar{\kappa}_{*}\bar{Z}_{1}+\sigma_{1}\eta_{j})^{2}-\Big(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1}{k}$}}\msum_{j=1}^{k}\big(u^{\prime\prime}_{j}-\alpha\bar{\kappa}_{*}\bar{Z}_{1}+\sigma_{1}\eta_{j}\big)\Big)^{2}\Big)\bigg]\;,

where
\eta_{1},\ldots,\eta_{k}
and
\bar{Z}_{2}
are i.i.d. standard normals and
\varepsilon_{1}
is an independent
\textrm{Logistic}(0,1)
variable.. Notice that
u^{\prime}\in(P_{mk}(S_{u}))_{1}
has equal entries, say
u_{0}
, and
u^{\prime\prime}\in(P^{\perp}_{mk}(S_{u}))_{1}
satisfies
\sum_{j=1}^{k}u^{\prime\prime}_{j}=0
. Also recall the assumption that
\sup_{u\in S_{u}}\frac{\|u\|^{2}_{2}}{mk}\rightarrow\infty
. Setting
\tilde{u}=(u^{\prime\prime}_{1}+u_{0},\ldots,u^{\prime\prime}_{k}+u_{0})
, the above can be further approximated by

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\bigg[

\displaystyle\min_{\tilde{u}\in\mathbb{R}^{k}}\;\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1}{k}$}}\msum_{j=1}^{k}\log(1+e^{\tilde u_j})

\displaystyle\;+\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{r_2 \nu_2}{2}$}}\Big(\,\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1}{k}$}}\msum_{j\leq k}\Big(\tilde{u}_{j}-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1}{r_2 \nu_2}$}}\mathbb{I}_{\geq 0}\{\bar{\kappa}_{o}\bar{Z}_{0}+\bar{\kappa}_{*}\bar{Z}_{1}-\varepsilon_{1}\}-\alpha\bar{\kappa}_{*}\bar{Z}_{1}+\sigma_{1}\eta_{j}+\sigma_{2}\bar{Z}_{2}\Big)\,\Big)^{2}

\displaystyle\;+\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{r_1 \nu_1}{2}$}}\,\Big(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1}{k}$}}\msum_{j=1}^{k}(\tilde{u}_{j}-\alpha\bar{\kappa}_{*}\bar{Z}_{1}+\sigma_{1}\eta_{j})^{2}-\Big(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1}{k}$}}\msum_{j=1}^{k}\big(\tilde{u}_{j}-\alpha\bar{\kappa}_{*}\bar{Z}_{1}+\sigma_{1}\eta_{j}\big)\Big)^{2}\Big)\bigg]

\displaystyle\;=\;\mathbb{E}\bigg[

\displaystyle\min_{\tilde{u}\in\mathbb{R}^{k}}\;\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1}{k}$}}\mathbf{1}_{k}^{\intercal}\rho(\tilde{u})+\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{r_1 \nu_1}{2 k}$}}\,\Big\|\big(I_{k}-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1}{k}$}}\mathbf{1}_{k\times k}\big)(\tilde{u}-\alpha\bar{\kappa}_{*}\bar{Z}_{1}\mathbf{1}_{k}+\sigma_{1}\eta)\Big\|^{2}

\displaystyle\;+\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{r_2 \nu_2}{2 k}$}}\Big\|\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1}{k}$}}\mathbf{1}_{k\times k}\Big(\tilde{u}-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1}{r_2 \nu_2}$}}\mathbb{I}_{\geq 0}\{\bar{\kappa}_{o}\bar{Z}_{0}+\bar{\kappa}_{*}\bar{Z}_{1}-\varepsilon_{1}\}\mathbf{1}_{k}-\alpha\bar{\kappa}_{*}\bar{Z}_{1}\mathbf{1}_{k}+\sigma_{1}\eta+\sigma_{2}\bar{Z}_{2}\mathbf{1}_{k}\Big)\Big\|^{2}\bigg]\;,

which equals
\bar{M}_{\rho}^{r,\nu,\alpha,\sigma}
. Substituting this into (
134
) while also applying the assumption that
\sup_{v\in S_{v}}\frac{\|v\|^{2}_{2}}{mk}\rightarrow\infty
, we obtain

\displaystyle\min_{\begin{subarray}{c}\alpha\in S^{\alpha}\\
(\sigma_{1},\sigma_{2})\in S_{\sigma_{1}}\times S_{\sigma_{2}}\\
\nu_{1},\nu_{2}\geq 0\end{subarray}}\,\max_{\begin{subarray}{c}(r_{1},r_{2})\in S_{r_{1}}\times S_{r_{2}}\\
\tau_{1},\tau_{2}\geq 0\\
\theta\in\mathbb{R}\end{subarray}}\,

\displaystyle\,-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\sigma_1}{2 \tau_1}$}}-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\sigma_2}{2 \tau_2}$}}+\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{r_1}{2 \nu_1}$}}+\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{r_2}{2 \nu_2}$}}+\alpha\theta\bar{\kappa}_{*}^{2}-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\alpha^2 \bar\kappa_*^2}{2 \sigma_2 \tau_2}$}}

\displaystyle\,-\bar{\chi}^{r,\theta,\sigma,\tau}_{1}+\epsilon_{S}^{2}\,\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\bar\chi^{r, \theta, \sigma, \tau}_3}{\bar\chi^{r, \theta, \sigma, \tau}_2}$}}-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1}{4 r_2 \nu_2}$}}-\alpha\,\mathbb{E}\big[\sigma(\bar{\kappa}_{o}\bar{Z}_{0}+\bar{\kappa}_{*}\bar{Z}_{1})\bar{\kappa}_{*}\bar{Z}_{1}\big]+\bar{M}_{\rho}^{r,\nu,\alpha,\sigma}\;.

\jmlrQED

N.4
Proof of
51
: (
DO
) to (
EQs
)

Since
S=\mathcal{S}_{p}
, we can ignore terms involving
\bar{\chi}^{r,\theta,\sigma,\tau}_{2}
and
\bar{\chi}^{r,\theta,\sigma,\tau}_{3}
. Setting the first derivative of (
DO
) to zero with respect to each variable, we obtain

\displaystyle\begin{cases}0\;=\;\theta\bar{\kappa}_{*}^{2}-\frac{\alpha\bar{\kappa}_{*}^{2}}{\sigma_{2}\tau_{2}}-\mathbb{E}\big[\sigma(\bar{\kappa}_{o}\bar{Z}_{0}+\bar{\kappa}_{*}\bar{Z}_{1})\bar{\kappa}_{*}\bar{Z}_{1}\big]+\partial_{\alpha}\bar{M}_{\rho}^{r,\nu,\alpha,\sigma}\;,\\
0\;=\;-\frac{1}{2\tau_{1}}-\partial_{\sigma_{1}}\bar{\chi}_{1}^{r,\theta,\sigma,\tau}+\partial_{\sigma_{1}}\bar{M}_{\rho}^{r,\nu,\alpha,\sigma}\;,\\
0\;=\;-\frac{1}{2\tau_{2}}+\frac{\alpha^{2}\bar{\kappa}_{*}^{2}}{2\sigma_{2}^{2}\tau_{2}}-\partial_{\sigma_{2}}\bar{\chi}_{1}^{r,\theta,\sigma,\tau}+\partial_{\sigma_{2}}\bar{M}_{\rho}^{r,\nu,\alpha,\sigma}\;,\\
0\;=\;\frac{\sigma_{1}}{2\tau_{1}^{2}}-\partial_{\tau_{1}}\bar{\chi}_{1}^{r,\theta,\sigma,\tau}\;,\\
0\;=\;\frac{\sigma_{2}}{2\tau_{2}^{2}}+\frac{\alpha^{2}\bar{\kappa}_{*}^{2}}{2\sigma_{2}\tau_{2}^{2}}-\partial_{\tau_{2}}\bar{\chi}_{1}^{r,\theta,\sigma,\tau}\;,\\
0\;=\;-\frac{r_{1}}{2\nu_{1}^{2}}+\partial_{\nu_{1}}\bar{M}_{\rho}^{r,\nu,\alpha,\sigma}\;,\\
0\;=\;-\frac{r_{2}}{2\nu_{2}^{2}}+\frac{1}{4r_{2}\nu_{2}^{2}}+\partial_{\nu_{2}}\bar{M}_{\rho}^{r,\nu,\alpha,\sigma}\;,\\
0\;=\;\frac{1}{2\nu_{1}}-\partial_{r_{1}}\bar{\chi}_{1}^{r,\theta,\sigma,\tau}+\partial_{r_{1}}\bar{M}_{\rho}^{r,\nu,\alpha,\sigma}\;,\\
0\;=\;\frac{1}{2\nu_{2}}+\frac{1}{4r_{2}^{2}\nu_{2}}-\partial_{r_{2}}\bar{\chi}_{1}^{r,\theta,\sigma,\tau}+\partial_{r_{2}}\bar{M}_{\rho}^{r,\nu,\alpha,\sigma}\;,\\
0\;=\;\alpha\bar{\kappa}_{*}^{2}-\partial_{\theta}\bar{\chi}_{1}^{r,\theta,\sigma,\tau}\;.\end{cases}

(135)

The next step is to compute the derivatives of

\displaystyle\bar{M}_{\rho}^{r,\nu,\alpha,\sigma}\;\coloneqq\;

\displaystyle\;\mathbb{E}\bigg[\min_{\tilde{u}\in\mathbb{R}^{k}}\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1}{k}$}}\mathbf{1}_{k}^{\intercal}\rho(\tilde{u})+\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{r_1 \nu_1}{2 k}$}}\,\big\|\big(I_{k}-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1}{k}$}}\mathbf{1}_{k\times k}\big)(\tilde{u}+\sigma_{1}\eta)\big\|^{2}

\displaystyle\qquad+\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{r_2 \nu_2}{2 k}$}}\Big\|\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1}{k}$}}\mathbf{1}_{k\times k}\Big(\tilde{u}-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1}{r_2 \nu_2}$}}\mathbb{I}_{\geq 0}\{\bar{\kappa}_{o}\bar{Z}_{0}+\bar{\kappa}_{*}\bar{Z}_{1}-\varepsilon_{1}\}\mathbf{1}_{k}-\alpha\bar{\kappa}_{*}\bar{Z}_{1}\mathbf{1}_{k}+\sigma_{1}\eta+\sigma_{2}\bar{Z}_{2}\mathbf{1}_{k}\Big)\Big\|^{2}\bigg]\;.

Recall that we denote
u_{\bar{Z},\varepsilon_{1},\eta}
as the minimizer of the minimization inside the expectation. By the envelope theorem and noting that
\mathbb{E}[\mathbb{I}_{\geq 0}\{\bar{\kappa}_{o}\bar{Z}_{0}+\bar{\kappa}_{*}\bar{Z}_{1}-\varepsilon_{1}\}]=\mathbb{E}[\sigma(\bar{\kappa}_{o}\bar{Z}_{0}+\bar{\kappa}_{*}\bar{Z}_{1})]
, we have

\displaystyle\partial_{\alpha}\bar{M}_{\rho}^{r,\nu,\alpha,\sigma}\;=

\displaystyle\;-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{r_2 \nu_2 \bar\kappa_* }{k}$}}\mathbb{E}\,\Big[\bar{Z}_{1}\Big(\mathbf{1}_{k}^{\intercal}u_{\bar{Z},\varepsilon_{1},\eta}-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{k}{r_2 \nu_2}$}}\sigma(\bar{\kappa}_{o}\bar{Z}_{0}+\bar{\kappa}_{*}\bar{Z}_{1})\Big)\Big]+r_{2}\nu_{2}\alpha\bar{\kappa}_{*}^{2}\;,

\displaystyle\partial_{\sigma_{1}}\bar{M}_{\rho}^{r,\nu,\alpha,\sigma}\;=

\displaystyle\;\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{r_1 \nu_1}{ k}$}}\,\mathbb{E}\Big[\eta^{\intercal}\,\big(I_{k}-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1}{k}$}}\mathbf{1}_{k\times k}\big)\,u_{\bar{Z},\varepsilon_{1},\eta}\Big]+\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{r_1 \nu_1 \sigma_1 (k-1)}{ k}$}}+\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{r_2 \nu_2 }{ k}$}}\mathbb{E}\Big[\eta^{\intercal}\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1}{k}$}}\mathbf{1}_{k\times k}u_{\bar{Z},\varepsilon_{1},\eta}\bigg]+\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{r_2 \nu_2\sigma_1 }{ k}$}}\;,

\displaystyle\partial_{\sigma_{2}}\bar{M}_{\rho}^{r,\nu,\alpha,\sigma}\;=

\displaystyle\;\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{r_2 \nu_2 }{ k}$}}\mathbb{E}\Big[\bar{Z}_{2}\mathbf{1}_{k}^{\intercal}u_{\bar{Z},\varepsilon_{1},\eta}\Big]+r_{2}\nu_{2}\sigma_{2}\;,

\displaystyle\partial_{\nu_{1}}\bar{M}_{\rho}^{r,\nu,\alpha,\sigma}\;=

\displaystyle\;\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{r_1}{2 k}$}}\,\mathbb{E}\Big[\big\|\big(I_{k}-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1}{k}$}}\mathbf{1}_{k\times k}\big)(u_{\bar{Z},\varepsilon_{1},\eta}+\sigma_{1}\eta)\big\|^{2}\Big]\;,

\displaystyle\partial_{\nu_{2}}\bar{M}_{\rho}^{r,\nu,\alpha,\sigma}\;=

\displaystyle\;\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{r_2}{2 k}$}}\mathbb{E}\bigg[\Big\|\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1}{k}$}}\mathbf{1}_{k\times k}\Big(u_{\bar{Z},\varepsilon_{1},\eta}-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1}{r_2 \nu_2}$}}\mathbb{I}_{\geq 0}\{\bar{\kappa}_{o}\bar{Z}_{0}+\bar{\kappa}_{*}\bar{Z}_{1}-\varepsilon_{1}\}\mathbf{1}_{k}-\alpha\bar{\kappa}_{*}\bar{Z}_{1}\mathbf{1}_{k}+\sigma_{1}\eta+\sigma_{2}\bar{Z}_{2}\mathbf{1}_{k}\Big)\Big\|^{2}\bigg]

\displaystyle\;+\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1}{\nu_2 k}$}}\mathbb{E}\bigg[\mathbb{I}_{\geq 0}\{\bar{\kappa}_{o}\bar{Z}_{0}+\bar{\kappa}_{*}\bar{Z}_{1}-\varepsilon_{1}\}\Big(\mathbf{1}_{k}^{\intercal}u_{\bar{Z},\varepsilon_{1},\eta}-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{k}{r_2 \nu_2}$}}\sigma(\bar{\kappa}_{o}\bar{Z}_{0}+\bar{\kappa}_{*}\bar{Z}_{1})-k\alpha\bar{\kappa}_{*}\bar{Z}_{1}\Big)\bigg]\;,

\displaystyle\partial_{r_{1}}\bar{M}_{\rho}^{r,\nu,\alpha,\sigma}\;=

\displaystyle\;\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\nu_1}{2 k}$}}\,\mathbb{E}\Big[\big\|\big(I_{k}-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1}{k}$}}\mathbf{1}_{k\times k}\big)(u_{\bar{Z},\varepsilon_{1},\eta}+\sigma_{1}\eta)\big\|^{2}\Big]\;,

\displaystyle\partial_{r_{2}}\bar{M}_{\rho}^{r,\nu,\alpha,\sigma}\;=

\displaystyle\;\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\nu_2}{2 k}$}}\mathbb{E}\bigg[\Big\|\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1}{k}$}}\mathbf{1}_{k\times k}\Big(u_{\bar{Z},\varepsilon_{1},\eta}-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1}{r_2 \nu_2}$}}\mathbb{I}_{\geq 0}\{\bar{\kappa}_{o}\bar{Z}_{0}+\bar{\kappa}_{*}\bar{Z}_{1}-\varepsilon_{1}\}\mathbf{1}_{k}-\alpha\bar{\kappa}_{*}\bar{Z}_{1}\mathbf{1}_{k}+\sigma_{1}\eta+\sigma_{2}\bar{Z}_{2}\mathbf{1}_{k}\Big)\Big\|^{2}\bigg]

\displaystyle\;+\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1}{r_2 k}$}}\mathbb{E}\bigg[\mathbb{I}_{\geq 0}\{\bar{\kappa}_{o}\bar{Z}_{0}+\bar{\kappa}_{*}\bar{Z}_{1}-\varepsilon_{1}\}\Big(\mathbf{1}_{k}^{\intercal}u_{\bar{Z},\varepsilon_{1},\eta}-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{k}{r_2 \nu_2}$}}\sigma(\bar{\kappa}_{o}\bar{Z}_{0}+\bar{\kappa}_{*}\bar{Z}_{1})-k\alpha\bar{\kappa}_{*}\bar{Z}_{1}\Big)\bigg]\;.

Writing
\bar{Y}=\mathbb{I}_{\geq 0}\{\bar{\kappa}_{o}\bar{Z}_{0}+\bar{\kappa}_{*}\bar{Z}_{1}-\varepsilon_{1}\}
and substituting the bounds above into the system of equations recovers (

).

N.5.1
Proof of
52
: isotropic, no augmentation

Under the stated setup, the covariance matrices in the formula evaluate to
\Sigma_{o}=\Sigma=\frac{1}{p}I_{p}
and
\Sigma_{*}=I_{p}
. In this case, as
m=n,p\rightarrow\infty
and
p/n\rightarrow\kappa
, we can compute the limit terms defined in (
DO
):

\displaystyle\;\bar{\kappa}_{*}\;=\;\lim_{p\rightarrow\infty}\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\| \beta^* \|}{\sqrt{p}}$}}\;,\qquad\bar{\kappa}_{o}\;=\;\bar{\chi}^{\sigma,\tau}_{11}\;=\;0\;,\qquad\bar{\chi}^{\sigma,\tau}_{12}\;=\;\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\kappa}{\sigma_2 \tau_2 \lambda\kappa+ 1}$}}\;,\qquad\bar{\chi}^{\sigma,\tau}_{13}\;=\;\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1}{\sigma_2 \tau_2 \lambda\kappa+ 1}$}}\;,

\displaystyle\;\bar{\chi}^{\sigma,\tau}_{21}\;=\;0\;,\qquad\bar{\chi}^{\sigma,\tau}_{22}\;=\;\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\kappa}{(\sigma_2 \tau_2 \lambda\kappa+ 1)^2}$}}\;,\qquad\bar{\chi}^{\sigma,\tau}_{23}\;=\;\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1}{(\sigma_2 \tau_2 \lambda\kappa+ 1)^2}$}}\;.

This implies

\displaystyle\bar{\chi}_{1}^{r,\theta,\sigma,\tau}\;=\;\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{r_2^2 \kappa+ \theta^2 \bar\kappa_*^2 }{2 ( \lambda\kappa+ \sigma_2^{-1} \tau_2^{-1} )}$}}\;,\qquad\bar{\chi}_{2}^{r,\theta,\sigma,\tau}\;=\;\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{r_2^2 \kappa+
\theta^2 \bar\kappa_*^2
}{(\lambda\kappa+ \sigma_2^{-1} \tau_2^{-1} )^2}$}}\;,

which are in particular independent of
\sigma_{1}
,
\tau_{1}
and
r_{1}
. Now recall that (
EQs
) read

\displaystyle\begin{cases}0\;=\;\theta\bar{\kappa}_{*}^{2}-\frac{\alpha\bar{\kappa}_{*}^{2}}{\sigma_{2}\tau_{2}}-\frac{r_{2}\nu_{2}\bar{\kappa}_{*}}{k}\mathbb{E}\,\Big[\bar{Z}_{1}\mathbf{1}_{k}^{\intercal}u_{\bar{Z},\varepsilon_{1},\eta}\Big]+r_{2}\nu_{2}\alpha\bar{\kappa}_{*}^{2}\;,\\
0\;=\;-\frac{1}{2\tau_{1}}-\partial_{\sigma_{1}}\bar{\chi}_{1}^{r,\theta,\sigma,\tau}+\frac{r_{1}\nu_{1}}{k}\,\mathbb{E}\Big[\eta^{\intercal}\,\big(I_{k}-\frac{1}{k}\mathbf{1}_{k\times k}\big)\,u_{\bar{Z},\varepsilon_{1},\eta}\Big]+\frac{r_{1}\nu_{1}\sigma_{1}(k-1)}{k}+\frac{r_{2}\nu_{2}}{k}\mathbb{E}\Big[\eta^{\intercal}\frac{1}{k}\mathbf{1}_{k\times k}u_{\bar{Z},\varepsilon_{1},\eta}\bigg]\\
\;\qquad+\frac{r_{2}\nu_{2}\sigma_{1}}{k}\;,\\
0\;=\;-\frac{1}{2\tau_{2}}+\frac{\alpha^{2}\bar{\kappa}_{*}^{2}}{2\sigma_{2}^{2}\tau_{2}}-\partial_{\sigma_{2}}\bar{\chi}_{1}^{r,\theta,\sigma,\tau}+\frac{r_{2}\nu_{2}}{k}\mathbb{E}\Big[\bar{Z}_{2}\mathbf{1}_{k}^{\intercal}u_{\bar{Z},\varepsilon_{1},\eta}\Big]+r_{2}\nu_{2}\sigma_{2}\;,\\
0\;=\;\frac{\sigma_{1}}{2\tau_{1}^{2}}-\partial_{\tau_{1}}\bar{\chi}_{1}^{r,\theta,\sigma,\tau}\;,\\

0\;=\;-\frac{r_{2}}{2\nu_{2}^{2}}+\frac{1}{4r_{2}\nu_{2}^{2}}+\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{r_2}{2 k}$}}\mathbb{E}\bigg[\Big\|\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1}{k}$}}\mathbf{1}_{k\times k}\Big(u_{\bar{Z},\varepsilon_{1},\eta}-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1}{r_2 \nu_2}$}}\bar{Y}\mathbf{1}_{k}-\alpha\bar{\kappa}_{*}\bar{Z}_{1}\mathbf{1}_{k}+\sigma_{1}\eta+\sigma_{2}\bar{Z}_{2}\mathbf{1}_{k}\Big)\Big\|^{2}\bigg]\\

0\;=\;\frac{1}{2\nu_{1}}-\partial_{r_{1}}\bar{\chi}_{1}^{r,\theta,\sigma,\tau}+\frac{\nu_{1}}{2k}\,\mathbb{E}\Big[\big\|\big(I_{k}-\frac{1}{k}\mathbf{1}_{k\times k}\big)(u_{\bar{Z},\varepsilon_{1},\eta}+\sigma_{1}\eta)\big\|^{2}\Big]\;,\\
0\;=\;\frac{1}{2\nu_{2}}+\frac{1}{4r_{2}^{2}\nu_{2}}-\partial_{r_{2}}\bar{\chi}_{1}^{r,\theta,\sigma,\tau}+\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\nu_2}{2 k}$}}\mathbb{E}\bigg[\Big\|\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1}{k}$}}\mathbf{1}_{k\times k}\Big(u_{\bar{Z},\varepsilon_{1},\eta}-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1}{r_2 \nu_2}$}}\bar{Y}\mathbf{1}_{k}-\alpha\bar{\kappa}_{*}\bar{Z}_{1}\mathbf{1}_{k}+\sigma_{1}\eta+\sigma_{2}\bar{Z}_{2}\mathbf{1}_{k}\Big)\Big\|^{2}\bigg]\\
\;\qquad+\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1}{r_2 k}$}}\mathbb{E}\bigg[\bar{Y}\Big(\mathbf{1}_{k}^{\intercal}u_{\bar{Z},\varepsilon_{1},\eta}-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{k}{r_2 \nu_2}$}}\bar{Y}-k\alpha\bar{\kappa}_{*}\bar{Z}_{1}\Big)\bigg]\;,\\
0\;=\;\alpha\bar{\kappa}_{*}^{2}-\partial_{\theta}\bar{\chi}_{1}^{r,\theta,\sigma,\tau}\;.\end{cases}

By the 4th equation,
\sigma_{1}=0
. In this case, the defining optimization of
u_{\bar{Z},\varepsilon_{1},\eta}
is symmetric under permutation of
\tilde{u}\in\mathbb{R}^{k}
and in particular
\frac{1}{k}\mathbf{1}_{k\times k}u_{\bar{Z},\varepsilon_{1},\eta}=u_{\bar{Z},\varepsilon_{1},\eta}
. This implies that
u_{\bar{Z},\varepsilon_{1},\eta}=u_{\bar{Z},\varepsilon_{1}}\mathbf{1}_{k}
where
u_{\bar{Z},\varepsilon_{1}}
is the minimizer of the 1-d random optimization problem

\displaystyle\min_{\tilde{u}\in\mathbb{R}}\,\rho(\tilde{u})+\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{r_2 \nu_2}{2}$}}\big(\tilde{u}-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1}{r_2 \nu_2}$}}\bar{Y}-\alpha\bar{\kappa}_{*}\bar{Z}_{1}+\sigma_{2}\bar{Z}_{2}\big)^{2}\;.

(136)

Recall that
{\rm Prox}_{t\rho({\,\vbox{\hbox{\tiny$\bullet$}}\,})}(v)\coloneqq\argmin_{x\in\mathbb{R}}\frac{1}{2t}(v-x)^{2}+\rho(x)
. This allows us to express

\displaystyle u_{\bar{Z},\varepsilon_{1}}\;=

\displaystyle\;\textrm{Prox}_{(r_{2}\nu_{2})^{-1}\rho({\,\vbox{\hbox{\tiny$\bullet$}}\,})}\Big(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1}{r_2 \nu_2}$}}\bar{Y}+\alpha\bar{\kappa}_{*}\bar{Z}_{1}-\sigma_{2}\bar{Z}_{2}\Big)\;.

Meanwhile, substituting
(I_{k}-\frac{1}{k}\mathbf{1}_{k\times k})u_{\bar{Z},\varepsilon_{1},\eta}=0
into the 6th and 8th equations above yields
r_{1}=0
and
\nu_{1}\rightarrow\infty
. We can WLOG take
\nu_{1}\rightarrow\infty
such that
r_{1}\nu_{1}\rightarrow 0
. By the 2nd equation we then obtain

\displaystyle\tau_{1}\;=\;\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1}{2}$}}\Big(\frac{r_{2}\nu_{2}}{k}\mathbb{E}\big[\eta^{\intercal}\mathbf{1}_{k}u_{\bar{Z},\varepsilon}\big]\Big)^{-1}\;\rightarrow\;\infty\;,

by noting that
\eta
is zero-mean and independent of
u_{\bar{Z},\varepsilon}
. This removes
(\sigma_{1},r_{1},\nu_{1},\tau_{1})
from the equations. Substituting
u_{\bar{Z},\varepsilon_{1},\eta}=\bar{u}_{\bar{Z},\varepsilon_{1}}\mathbf{1}_{k}
and the derivatives of
\bar{\chi}_{1}^{r,\theta,\sigma,\tau}
, we obtain

\displaystyle\begin{cases}0\;=\;\theta\bar{\kappa}_{*}^{2}-\frac{\alpha\bar{\kappa}_{*}^{2}}{\sigma_{2}\tau_{2}}-r_{2}\nu_{2}\bar{\kappa}_{*}\mathbb{E}\,\big[\bar{Z}_{1}u_{\bar{Z},\varepsilon_{1}}\big]+r_{2}\nu_{2}\alpha\bar{\kappa}_{*}^{2}\;,\\
0\;=\;-\frac{1}{2\tau_{2}}+\frac{\alpha^{2}\bar{\kappa}_{*}^{2}}{2\sigma_{2}^{2}\tau_{2}}-\frac{1}{\sigma_{2}^{2}\tau_{2}}\,\frac{r_{2}^{2}\kappa+\theta^{2}\bar{\kappa}_{*}^{2}}{2(\lambda\kappa+\sigma_{2}^{-1}\tau_{2}^{-1})^{2}}+r_{2}\nu_{2}\mathbb{E}\big[\bar{Z}_{2}u_{\bar{Z},\varepsilon_{1}}\big]+r_{2}\nu_{2}\sigma_{2}\;,\\
0\;=\;\frac{\sigma_{2}}{2\tau_{2}^{2}}+\frac{\alpha^{2}\bar{\kappa}_{*}^{2}}{2\sigma_{2}\tau_{2}^{2}}-\frac{1}{\sigma_{2}\tau_{2}^{2}}\,\frac{r_{2}^{2}\kappa+\theta^{2}\bar{\kappa}_{*}^{2}}{2(\lambda\kappa+\sigma_{2}^{-1}\tau_{2}^{-1})^{2}}\;,\\
0\;=\;-\frac{r_{2}}{2\nu_{2}^{2}}+\frac{1}{4r_{2}\nu_{2}^{2}}+\frac{r_{2}}{2}\mathbb{E}\big[\big(u_{\bar{Z},\varepsilon_{1}}-\frac{1}{r_{2}\nu_{2}}\bar{Y}-\alpha\bar{\kappa}_{*}\bar{Z}_{1}+\sigma_{2}\bar{Z}_{2}\big)^{2}\big]+\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1}{\nu_2}$}}\mathbb{E}\Big[\bar{Y}\Big(u_{\bar{Z},\varepsilon_{1}}-\frac{1}{r_{2}\nu_{2}}\bar{Y}-\alpha\bar{\kappa}_{*}\bar{Z}_{1}\Big)\Big]\;,\\
0\;=\;\frac{1}{2\nu_{2}}+\frac{1}{4r_{2}^{2}\nu_{2}}-\frac{r_{2}\kappa}{\lambda\kappa+\sigma_{2}^{-1}\tau_{2}^{-1}}+\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\nu_2}{2}$}}\mathbb{E}\Big[\big(u_{\bar{Z},\varepsilon_{1},\eta}-\frac{1}{r_{2}\nu_{2}}\bar{Y}-\alpha\bar{\kappa}_{*}\bar{Z}_{1}+\sigma_{2}\bar{Z}_{2}\big)^{2}\Big]\\
\;\qquad+\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1}{r_2}$}}\mathbb{E}\Big[\bar{Y}\Big(u_{\bar{Z},\varepsilon_{1},\eta}-\frac{1}{r_{2}\nu_{2}}\bar{Y}-\alpha\bar{\kappa}_{*}\bar{Z}_{1}\Big)\Big]\;,\\
0\;=\;\alpha\bar{\kappa}_{*}^{2}-\frac{\theta\bar{\kappa}_{*}^{2}}{\lambda\kappa+\sigma_{2}^{-1}\tau_{2}^{-1}}\;.\end{cases}

(137)

Now let
\gamma=\frac{1}{r_{2}\nu_{2}}
. Notice that the 4th and 5th equations above both involve

\displaystyle(\star)\;\coloneqq

\displaystyle\;\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1}{2}$}}\mathbb{E}\big[\big(u_{\bar{Z},\varepsilon_{1}}-\gamma\bar{Y}-\alpha\bar{\kappa}_{*}\bar{Z}_{1}+\sigma_{2}\bar{Z}_{2}\big)^{2}\big]+\gamma\mathbb{E}\Big[\bar{Y}\Big(u_{\bar{Z},\varepsilon_{1}}-\gamma\bar{Y}-\alpha\bar{\kappa}_{*}\bar{Z}_{1}\Big)\Big]

\displaystyle\;\overset{(a)}{=}

\displaystyle\;\mathbb{E}\bigg[\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1 - \bar Y}{2}$}}\big(\textrm{Prox}_{\gamma\rho({\,\vbox{\hbox{\tiny$\bullet$}}\,})}\big(\alpha\bar{\kappa}_{*}\bar{Z}_{1}-\sigma_{2}\bar{Z}_{2}\big)-\alpha\bar{\kappa}_{*}\bar{Z}_{1}+\sigma_{2}\bar{Z}_{2}\big)^{2}\bigg]

\displaystyle\;+\mathbb{E}\bigg[\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\bar Y}{2}$}}\big(\textrm{Prox}_{\gamma\rho({\,\vbox{\hbox{\tiny$\bullet$}}\,})}\big(\gamma+\alpha\bar{\kappa}_{*}\bar{Z}_{1}-\sigma_{2}\bar{Z}_{2}\big)-\gamma-\alpha\bar{\kappa}_{*}\bar{Z}_{1}+\sigma_{2}\bar{Z}_{2}\big)^{2}\bigg]

\displaystyle\;+\gamma\mathbb{E}\Big[\bar{Y}\Big(\textrm{Prox}_{\gamma\rho({\,\vbox{\hbox{\tiny$\bullet$}}\,})}\big(\gamma\bar{Y}+\alpha\bar{\kappa}_{*}\bar{Z}_{1}-\sigma_{2}\bar{Z}_{2}\big)-\gamma-\alpha\bar{\kappa}_{*}\bar{Z}_{1}\Big)\Big]

\displaystyle\;\overset{(b)}{=}

\displaystyle\;\mathbb{E}\bigg[\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\partial\rho( - \bar\kappa_* \bar Z_1 )}{2}$}}\big(\textrm{Prox}_{\gamma\rho({\,\vbox{\hbox{\tiny$\bullet$}}\,})}\big(\alpha\bar{\kappa}_{*}\bar{Z}_{1}-\sigma_{2}\bar{Z}_{2}\big)-\alpha\bar{\kappa}_{*}\bar{Z}_{1}+\sigma_{2}\bar{Z}_{2}\big)^{2}\bigg]

\displaystyle\;+\mathbb{E}\bigg[\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\partial\rho(\bar\kappa_* \bar Z_1)}{2}$}}\big(\textrm{Prox}_{\gamma\rho({\,\vbox{\hbox{\tiny$\bullet$}}\,})}\big(\gamma+\alpha\bar{\kappa}_{*}\bar{Z}_{1}-\sigma_{2}\bar{Z}_{2}\big)-\gamma-\alpha\bar{\kappa}_{*}\bar{Z}_{1}+\sigma_{2}\bar{Z}_{2}\big)^{2}\bigg]

\displaystyle\;-\gamma\mathbb{E}\Big[\partial\rho(\bar{\kappa}_{*}\bar{Z}_{1})\Big(\alpha\bar{\kappa}_{*}\bar{Z}_{1}+\gamma-\textrm{Prox}_{\gamma\rho({\,\vbox{\hbox{\tiny$\bullet$}}\,})}\big(\gamma+\alpha\bar{\kappa}_{*}\bar{Z}_{1}-\sigma_{2}\bar{Z}_{2}\big)\Big)\Big]

\displaystyle\;\overset{(c)}{=}

\displaystyle\;\mathbb{E}\bigg[\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\partial\rho( - \bar\kappa_* \bar Z_1 )}{2}$}}\big(\textrm{Prox}_{\gamma\rho({\,\vbox{\hbox{\tiny$\bullet$}}\,})}\big(\alpha\bar{\kappa}_{*}\bar{Z}_{1}-\sigma_{2}\bar{Z}_{2}\big)-\alpha\bar{\kappa}_{*}\bar{Z}_{1}+\sigma_{2}\bar{Z}_{2}\big)^{2}\bigg]

\displaystyle\;+\mathbb{E}\Big[\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\partial\rho(\bar\kappa_* \bar Z_1)}{2}$}}\big(\textrm{Prox}_{\gamma\rho({\,\vbox{\hbox{\tiny$\bullet$}}\,})}\big(\gamma+\alpha\bar{\kappa}_{*}\bar{Z}_{1}-\sigma_{2}\bar{Z}_{2}\big)-\alpha\bar{\kappa}_{*}\bar{Z}_{1}+\sigma_{2}\bar{Z}_{2}\big)^{2}\Big]-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\gamma^2}{2}$}}+\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\gamma^2}{4}$}}

\displaystyle\;\overset{(d)}{=}

\displaystyle\;\mathbb{E}\Big[\,\partial\rho(-\bar{\kappa}_{*}\bar{Z}_{1})\,\big(\alpha\bar{\kappa}_{*}\bar{Z}_{1}+\sigma_{2}\bar{Z}_{2}-\textrm{Prox}_{\gamma\rho({\,\vbox{\hbox{\tiny$\bullet$}}\,})}\big(\alpha\bar{\kappa}_{*}\bar{Z}_{1}+\sigma_{2}\bar{Z}_{2}\big)\big)^{2}\Big]-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\gamma^2}{4}$}}\;.

In
(a)
above, we have recalled that
\bar{Y}=\mathbb{I}_{\geq 0}\{\bar{\kappa}_{o}\bar{Z}_{0}+\bar{\kappa}_{*}\bar{Z}_{1}-\varepsilon_{1}\}=\mathbb{I}_{\geq 0}\{\bar{\kappa}_{*}\bar{Z}_{1}-\varepsilon_{1}\}
is an indicator function; in
(b)
we have noted the equality of the conditional distributions
1-\mathbb{I}_{\geq 0}\{\bar{\kappa}_{*}\bar{Z}_{1}-\varepsilon_{1}\}\,|\,\bar{Z}_{1}\overset{d}{=}\mathbb{I}_{\geq 0}\{-\bar{\kappa}_{*}\bar{Z}_{1}-\varepsilon_{1}\}\,|\,\bar{Z}_{1}
by the symmetry of
\varepsilon_{1}
followed by
\sigma({\,\vbox{\hbox{\tiny$\bullet$}}\,})=\partial\rho({\,\vbox{\hbox{\tiny$\bullet$}}\,})
; in
(c)
we have expanded the square in the second term and noted that
\mathbb{E}[\partial\rho(\bar{\kappa}_{*}\bar{Z}_{1})]=\mathbb{E}[(1+e^{-\bar{\kappa}_{*}\bar{Z}_{1}})^{-1}]=\frac{1}{2}
since
\bar{Z}_{1}
is symmetric about zero; in
(d)
, we have used in the second expectation that
\bar{Z}_{1}\overset{d}{=}-\bar{Z}_{1}
,
\bar{Z}_{2}\overset{d}{=}-\bar{Z}_{2}
and that

\displaystyle\textrm{Prox}_{\gamma\rho({\,\vbox{\hbox{\tiny$\bullet$}}\,})}\Big(\gamma+\alpha\bar{\kappa}_{*}\bar{Z}_{1}-\sigma_{2}\bar{Z}_{2}\Big)\;=\;-\textrm{Prox}_{\gamma\rho({\,\vbox{\hbox{\tiny$\bullet$}}\,})}\Big(-\alpha\bar{\kappa}_{*}\bar{Z}_{1}+\sigma_{2}\bar{Z}_{2}\Big)\;,

where we have used
\textrm{Prox}_{\gamma\rho({\,\vbox{\hbox{\tiny$\bullet$}}\,})}(x+\gamma)=-\textrm{Prox}_{\gamma\rho({\,\vbox{\hbox{\tiny$\bullet$}}\,})}(-x)
(see e.g. Lemma 3 of
Salehi et al. (2019)
). Substituting this into the last three lines of (
137
) gives

\displaystyle\begin{cases}\gamma^{2}\;=\;\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{2}{r_2^2}$}}\mathbb{E}\Big[\,\partial\rho(-\bar{\kappa}_{*}\bar{Z}_{1})\,\big(\bar{\kappa}_{*}\alpha\bar{Z}_{1}+\sigma_{2}\bar{Z}_{2}-\textrm{Prox}_{\gamma\rho({\,\vbox{\hbox{\tiny$\bullet$}}\,})}\big(\alpha\bar{\kappa}_{*}\bar{Z}_{1}+\sigma_{2}\bar{Z}_{2}\big)\big)^{2}\Big]\;,\\
\gamma\;=\;\frac{\kappa}{\lambda\kappa+\sigma_{2}^{-1}\tau_{2}^{-1}}\;,\\
\alpha\;=\;\frac{\theta}{\lambda\kappa+\sigma_{2}^{-1}\tau_{2}^{-1}}\;.\end{cases}

(138)

Meanwhile, the third line of (
137
) implies

\displaystyle\sigma_{2}^{2}+\alpha^{2}\bar{\kappa}_{*}^{2}\;=

\displaystyle\;\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{ r_2^2 \kappa+ \theta^2 \bar\kappa_*^2 }{(\lambda\kappa+ \sigma_2^{-1} \tau_2^{-1} )^2}$}}\;=\;\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{ r_2^2 \kappa}{(\lambda\kappa+ \sigma_2^{-1} \tau_2^{-1} )^2}$}}+\alpha^{2}\bar{\kappa}_{*}^{2}\;.

(139)

Combining the two calculations, we obtain

\displaystyle\theta\;=\;\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\alpha\kappa}{\gamma}$}}\;,\qquad\tau_{2}\;=\;\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\kappa^{-1} \gamma}{\sigma_2(1 - \gamma\lambda)}$}}\;,\qquad r_{2}\;=\;\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\sigma_2 \sqrt{\kappa}}{\gamma}$}}\;,

(140)

which gives the first three desired equations. Substituting these back into the first line of (
138
) gives

\displaystyle\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\sigma^2 \kappa}{2}$}}\;=\;\mathbb{E}\big[\partial\rho(-\bar{\kappa}_{*}\bar{Z}_{1})\big(\alpha\bar{\kappa}_{*}\bar{Z}_{1}+\sigma_{2}\bar{Z}_{2}-{\rm Prox}_{\gamma\rho({\,\vbox{\hbox{\tiny$\bullet$}}\,})}(\alpha\bar{\kappa}_{*}\bar{Z}_{1}+\sigma_{2}\bar{Z}_{2})\big)^{2}\big]\;,

(141)

which is the fourth desired equation. The first and second equations of (
137
) are handled similarly as appendix C.3 of
Salehi et al. (2019)
. We recall that
1-\mathbb{I}_{\geq 0}\{\bar{\kappa}_{*}\bar{Z}_{1}-\varepsilon_{1}\}\,|\,\bar{Z}_{1}\overset{d}{=}\mathbb{I}_{\geq 0}\{-\bar{\kappa}_{*}\bar{Z}_{1}-\varepsilon_{1}\}\,|\,\bar{Z}_{1}
and
\textrm{Prox}_{\gamma\rho({\,\vbox{\hbox{\tiny$\bullet$}}\,})}(x+\gamma)=-\textrm{Prox}_{\gamma\rho({\,\vbox{\hbox{\tiny$\bullet$}}\,})}(-x)
again to compute

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\big[\bar{Z}_{1}

\displaystyle\,u_{\bar{Z},\varepsilon_{1}}\big]\;=\;\mathbb{E}\big[\bar{Z}_{1}\textrm{Prox}_{\gamma\rho({\,\vbox{\hbox{\tiny$\bullet$}}\,})}\big(\gamma\bar{Y}+\alpha\bar{\kappa}_{*}\bar{Z}_{1}-\sigma_{2}\bar{Z}_{2}\big)\big]

\displaystyle\;=

\displaystyle\;\mathbb{E}\big[\bar{Z}_{1}\bar{Y}\textrm{Prox}_{\gamma\rho({\,\vbox{\hbox{\tiny$\bullet$}}\,})}\big(\gamma+\alpha\bar{\kappa}_{*}\bar{Z}_{1}-\sigma_{2}\bar{Z}_{2}\big)\big]+\mathbb{E}\big[\bar{Z}_{1}(1-\bar{Y})\textrm{Prox}_{\gamma\rho({\,\vbox{\hbox{\tiny$\bullet$}}\,})}\big(\alpha\bar{\kappa}_{*}\bar{Z}_{1}-\sigma_{2}\bar{Z}_{2}\big)\big]

\displaystyle\;=

\displaystyle\;\mathbb{E}\big[\bar{Z}_{1}\,\partial\rho(\bar{\kappa}_{*}\bar{Z}_{1})\,\textrm{Prox}_{\gamma\rho({\,\vbox{\hbox{\tiny$\bullet$}}\,})}\big(\gamma+\alpha\bar{\kappa}_{*}\bar{Z}_{1}-\sigma_{2}\bar{Z}_{2}\big)\big]+\mathbb{E}\big[\bar{Z}_{1}\,\partial\rho(-\bar{\kappa}_{*}\bar{Z}_{1})\,\textrm{Prox}_{\gamma\rho({\,\vbox{\hbox{\tiny$\bullet$}}\,})}\big(\alpha\bar{\kappa}_{*}\bar{Z}_{1}-\sigma_{2}\bar{Z}_{2}\big)\big]

\displaystyle\;=

\displaystyle\;-\mathbb{E}\big[\bar{Z}_{1}\,\partial\rho(\bar{\kappa}_{*}\bar{Z}_{1})\,\textrm{Prox}_{\gamma\rho({\,\vbox{\hbox{\tiny$\bullet$}}\,})}\big(-\alpha\bar{\kappa}_{*}\bar{Z}_{1}+\sigma_{2}\bar{Z}_{2}\big)\big]+\mathbb{E}\big[\bar{Z}_{1}\,\partial\rho(-\bar{\kappa}_{*}\bar{Z}_{1})\,\textrm{Prox}_{\gamma\rho({\,\vbox{\hbox{\tiny$\bullet$}}\,})}\big(\alpha\bar{\kappa}_{*}\bar{Z}_{1}-\sigma_{2}\bar{Z}_{2}\big)\big]

\displaystyle\;=

\displaystyle\;2\,\mathbb{E}\big[\bar{Z}_{1}\,\partial\rho(-\bar{\kappa}_{*}\bar{Z}_{1})\,\textrm{Prox}_{\gamma\rho({\,\vbox{\hbox{\tiny$\bullet$}}\,})}\big(\alpha\bar{\kappa}_{*}\bar{Z}_{1}-\sigma_{2}\bar{Z}_{2}\big)\big]

\displaystyle\;=

\displaystyle\;-2\,\mathbb{E}\big[\bar{\kappa}_{*}\partial^{2}\rho\big(-\bar{\kappa}_{*}\bar{Z}_{1})\,\textrm{Prox}_{\gamma\rho({\,\vbox{\hbox{\tiny$\bullet$}}\,})}\big(\alpha\bar{\kappa}_{*}\bar{Z}_{1}+\sigma_{2}\bar{Z}_{2}\big)\big]+\bar{\kappa}_{*}\alpha\,\mathbb{E}\bigg[\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\partial\rho(- \bar\kappa_* \bar Z_1)}{1+ \gamma\partial^2 \rho\big(\textrm{Prox}_{\gamma\rho(\argdot)}
\big(
\alpha\bar\kappa_* \bar Z_1
+
\sigma_2 \bar Z_2 \big) \big)}$}}\bigg]\;,

(142)

where the last line is exactly the same as (87)–(88) of
Salehi et al. (2019)
via Stein’s lemma and by noting that
\bar{Z}_{2}\overset{d}{=}-\bar{Z}_{2}
. Similarly

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\big[\bar{Z}_{2}u_{\bar{Z},\varepsilon_{1}}\big]=2\,\mathbb{E}\big[\bar{Z}_{2}\,\partial\rho(-\bar{\kappa}_{*}\bar{Z}_{1})\,\textrm{Prox}_{\gamma\rho({\,\vbox{\hbox{\tiny$\bullet$}}\,})}\big(\alpha\bar{\kappa}_{*}\bar{Z}_{1}-\sigma_{2}\bar{Z}_{2}\big)\big]=2\sigma_{2}\,\mathbb{E}\bigg[\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\partial\rho(- \bar\kappa_* \bar Z_1)}{1+ \gamma\partial^2 \rho\big(\textrm{Prox}_{\gamma\rho(\argdot)}
\big(
\alpha\bar\kappa_* \bar Z_1
+
\sigma_2 \bar Z_2 \big) \big)}$}}\bigg]\;,

(143)

where the last line is exactly the same as (83) of
Salehi et al. (2019)
via Stein’s lemma. Substituting (
143
) into the second equation of (
137
) gives

\displaystyle 0\;=\;-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1}{2 \tau_2}$}}+\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\alpha^2 \bar\kappa_*^2}{2 \sigma_2^2 \tau_2}$}}-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1}{\sigma_2^2 \tau_2}$}}\,\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{r_2^2 \kappa+ \theta^2 \bar\kappa_*^2 }{2 ( \lambda\kappa+ \sigma_2^{-1} \tau_2^{-1} )^2}$}}+\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{2 \sigma_2}{\gamma}$}}\,\mathbb{E}\bigg[\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\partial\rho(- \bar\kappa_* \bar Z_1)}{1+ \gamma\partial^2 \rho\big(\textrm{Prox}_{\gamma\rho(\argdot)}
\big(
\alpha\bar\kappa_* \bar Z_1
+
\sigma_2 \bar Z_2 \big) \big)}$}}\bigg]+\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\sigma_2 }{\gamma}$}}\;.

Upon rearranging and a substitution of
\sigma_{2}^{2}+\alpha^{2}\bar{\kappa}_{*}^{2}=\frac{r_{2}^{2}\kappa+\theta^{2}\bar{\kappa}_{*}^{2}}{(\lambda\kappa+\sigma_{2}^{-1}\tau_{2}^{-1})^{2}}
from (
139
) and
\tau_{2}\sigma_{2}=\frac{\kappa^{-1}\gamma}{1-\gamma\lambda}
from (
140
) , we obtain

\displaystyle 1-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\gamma}{\tau_2 \sigma_2}$}}\;=\;1-\kappa+\gamma\lambda\kappa\;=\;\mathbb{E}\bigg[\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\partial\rho(- \bar\kappa_* \bar Z_1)}{1+ \gamma\partial^2 \rho\big(\textrm{Prox}_{\gamma\rho(\argdot)}
\big(
\alpha\bar\kappa_* \bar Z_1
+
\sigma_2 \bar Z_2 \big) \big)}$}}\bigg]\;,

(144)

which gives the fifth desired equation. Substituting this into (
142
) implies

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\big[\bar{Z}_{1}u_{\bar{Z},\varepsilon_{1}}\big]\;=

\displaystyle\;-2\,\mathbb{E}\big[\bar{\kappa}_{*}\partial^{2}\rho\big(-\bar{\kappa}_{*}\bar{Z}_{1})\,\textrm{Prox}_{\gamma\rho({\,\vbox{\hbox{\tiny$\bullet$}}\,})}\big(\alpha\bar{\kappa}_{*}\bar{Z}_{1}+\sigma_{2}\bar{Z}_{2}\big)\big]+\bar{\kappa}_{*}\alpha-\bar{\kappa}_{*}\alpha\,\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\gamma}{\tau_2 \sigma_2}$}}\;,

and substituting this into the first equation of (
137
) gives

\displaystyle 0\;=\;\theta\bar{\kappa}_{*}^{2}-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{ \alpha\bar\kappa_*^2}{\sigma_2 \tau_2}$}}-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{ \bar\kappa_* }{\gamma}$}}\bigg(-2\,\mathbb{E}\big[\bar{\kappa}_{*}\partial^{2}\rho\big(-\bar{\kappa}_{*}\bar{Z}_{1})\,\textrm{Prox}_{\gamma\rho({\,\vbox{\hbox{\tiny$\bullet$}}\,})}\big(\alpha\bar{\kappa}_{*}\bar{Z}_{1}+\sigma_{2}\bar{Z}_{2}\big)\big]+\bar{\kappa}_{*}\alpha-\bar{\kappa}_{*}\alpha\,\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\gamma}{\tau_2 \sigma_2}$}}\bigg)+\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\alpha\bar\kappa_*^2 }{\gamma}$}}\;,

which simplifies to

\displaystyle-\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{\gamma\theta}{2}$}}\;=

\displaystyle\;\mathbb{E}[\partial^{2}\rho(-\bar{\kappa}_{*}\bar{Z}_{1}){\rm Prox}_{\gamma\rho({\,\vbox{\hbox{\tiny$\bullet$}}\,})}(\bar{\kappa}_{*}\alpha\bar{Z}_{1}+\sigma_{2}\bar{Z}_{2})]\;.

Replacing
\gamma\theta
by
\alpha\kappa
in view of (
140
) gives the last desired equation.

\jmlrQED

N.5.2
Proof of
54
: Random permutations

Since
Z_{1}\overset{d}{=}\phi_{1}(Z_{1})
,
Assumption

11
(i) holds. Now note that by the total law of covariance followed by that
\phi_{1}
and
\phi_{2}
are i.i.d.,

\displaystyle\text{\rm Cov}\,[\phi_{1}(Z_{1})\,,\,\phi_{2}(Z_{1})]\;=

\displaystyle\;\text{\rm Cov}\,[\mathbb{E}[\phi_{1}(Z_{1})\,|\,Z_{1}]\,,\,\mathbb{E}[\phi_{2}(Z_{1})\,|\,Z_{1}]]\,+\,\mathbb{E}\,[\text{\rm Cov}[\phi_{1}(Z_{1})\,,\,\phi_{2}(Z_{1})\,|\,Z_{1}]]

\displaystyle\;=

\displaystyle\;\text{\rm Var}\,\mathbb{E}[\phi_{1}(Z_{1})\,|\,Z_{1}]\;.

Denote
\tilde{p}_{t}=\lceil r_{\rm perm}p_{t}\rceil
, the number of fixed entries of the
l
-th group to be permuted. We can WLOG suppose they are chosen as the first
\tilde{p}_{t}
entries of the
t
-th group. Also write

Z^{(t)}_{t(\tilde{p}_{t}+1):tp_{t}}=(Z^{(t)}_{t(\tilde{p}_{t}+1)},\ldots,Z^{(t)}_{tp_{t}})^{\intercal}
, the vector of un-permuted coordinates within the
t
-th group. Then we may compute

\displaystyle\Sigma_{*}\;=

\displaystyle\;(\Sigma^{\dagger})^{1/2}\,\text{\rm Cov}\,[\phi_{1}(Z_{1})\,,\,\phi_{2}(Z_{1})]\,(\Sigma^{\dagger})^{1/2}

\displaystyle\;=

\displaystyle\;(\Sigma^{\dagger})^{1/2}\,\text{\rm Var}\,\mathbb{E}[\,\phi_{1}(Z_{1})\,|\,Z_{1}\,]\,(\Sigma^{\dagger})^{1/2}

\displaystyle\;=

\displaystyle\;(\Sigma^{\dagger})^{1/2}\text{\rm Var}\,\begin{pmatrix}\frac{1}{\tilde{p}_{1}}\sum_{l\leq p_{1}}Z^{(1)}_{1l}\times\mathbf{1}_{\tilde{p}_{1}}\\
Z^{(1)}_{1(\tilde{p}_{1}+1):1p_{1}}\\
\vdots\\
\frac{1}{\tilde{p}_{N}}\sum_{l\leq\tilde{p}_{N}}Z^{(N)}_{Nl}\times\mathbf{1}_{\tilde{p}_{N}}\\
Z^{(N)}_{N(\tilde{p}_{N}+1):Np_{N}}\end{pmatrix}\,(\Sigma^{\dagger})^{1/2}

\displaystyle\;=

\displaystyle\;(\Sigma^{\dagger})^{1/2}\begin{pmatrix}\frac{1}{\tilde{p}_{1}}\,\text{\rm Var}[Z^{(1)}_{11}]\times\mathbf{1}_{\tilde{p}_{1}\times\tilde{p}_{1}}&&&&\\
&\hskip-20.00003pt\text{\rm Var}[Z^{(1)}_{11}]\times I_{p-\tilde{p}_{1}}&&&\\
&&\hskip-20.00003pt\ddots&&\\
&&&\hskip-20.00003pt\frac{1}{\tilde{p}_{N}}\,\text{\rm Var}[Z^{(N)}_{11}]\times\mathbf{1}_{\tilde{p}_{N}\times\tilde{p}_{N}}&\\
&&&&\hskip-20.00003pt\text{\rm Var}[Z^{(N)}_{11}]\times I_{p-\tilde{p}_{N}}\end{pmatrix}(\Sigma^{\dagger})^{1/2}\;,

whereas

\displaystyle\Sigma\;=\;\Sigma_{o}\;=\;\text{\rm Var}[Z_{1}]\;=\;\begin{pmatrix}\text{\rm Var}[Z^{(1)}_{11}]\times I_{p_{1}}&&\\
&\ddots&\\
&&\text{\rm Var}[Z^{(N)}_{11}]\times I_{p_{N}}\end{pmatrix}\;,

and therefore

\displaystyle\Sigma_{*}\;=

\displaystyle\;\begin{pmatrix}\frac{1}{\tilde{p}_{1}}\,\mathbf{1}_{\tilde{p}_{1}\times\tilde{p}_{1}}\,\mathbb{I}\{\text{\rm Var}[Z^{(1)}_{11}]>0\}&&&&\\
&\hskip-20.00003ptI_{p-\tilde{p}_{1}}\,\mathbb{I}\{\text{\rm Var}[Z^{(1)}_{11}]>0\}&&&\\
&&\hskip-20.00003pt\ddots&&\\
&&&\hskip-20.00003pt\frac{1}{\tilde{p}_{N}}\,\mathbf{1}_{\tilde{p}_{N}\times\tilde{p}_{N}}\,\mathbb{I}\{\text{\rm Var}[Z^{(1)}_{11}]>0\}&\\
&&&&\hskip-20.00003ptI_{p-\tilde{p}_{N}}\,\mathbb{I}\{\text{\rm Var}[Z^{(1)}_{11}]>0\}\end{pmatrix}\;,

which satisfies
\Sigma_{*}^{2}=\Sigma_{*}
. Thus
Assumption

11
(ii) holds.

\jmlrQED

N.5.3
Proof of
55
: Random sign flipping

Since
\Sigma=\Sigma_{o}=\frac{1}{p}I_{p}
, we can write

\displaystyle\Sigma_{*}\;=

\displaystyle\;(\Sigma^{\dagger})^{1/2}\,\text{\rm Cov}[\phi_{1}(Z_{1})\,,\,\phi_{2}(Z_{1})]\,(\Sigma^{\dagger})^{1/2}\;=\;\mathbb{E}[\phi_{1}]\,\mathbb{E}[\phi_{2}]

and

\displaystyle(\Sigma^{\dagger})^{1/2}\,\text{\rm Cov}[\phi_{1}(Z_{1})\,,\,Z_{1}]\,(\Sigma_{o}^{\dagger})^{1/2}\;=\;\mathbb{E}[\phi_{1}]\;.

WLOG we can suppose that the
\lceil r_{\rm flip}p\rceil
entries are chosen as the first
\lceil r_{\rm flip}p\rceil
entries. Then each
\phi_{ij}=\textrm{diag}\{{\rm Rad}_{ij1},\ldots,{\rm Rad}_{ij\lceil r_{\rm flip}p\rceil},1,\ldots,1\}
, where
{\rm Rad}_{ijl}
’s are i.i.d. Rademacher random variables. Therefore
\mathbb{E}[\phi_{1}]=\textrm{diag}\{0,\ldots,0,1,\ldots,1\}
, where there are
\lceil r_{\rm flip}p\rceil
zeros, and in particular
\mathbb{E}[\phi_{1}]=\mathbb{E}[\phi_{1}]\mathbb{E}[\phi_{1}]=\mathbb{E}[\phi_{1}]\mathbb{E}[\phi_{2}^{\intercal}]
. This verifies both
Assumption

11
(i) and (ii).

\jmlrQED

N.5.4
Proof of
56
: Random cropping

In the random cropping setup,
\Sigma_{o}=\Sigma_{\rm new}=\frac{1}{p}I_{p}
. Also note that each
\phi_{i}
is a random projection matrix and independent of
Z_{i}
. Then by the total law of covariance,

\displaystyle\Sigma\;=\;\text{\rm Var}[\phi_{1}(Z_{1})]\;=

\displaystyle\;\mathbb{E}\text{\rm Var}[\phi_{1}(Z_{1})\,|\,\phi_{1}]+\text{\rm Var}\mathbb{E}[\phi_{1}(Z_{1})\,|\,\phi_{1}]

\displaystyle\;=

\displaystyle\;\mathbb{E}[\phi_{1}\text{\rm Var}[Z_{1}]\phi_{1}]+0\;=\;\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1}{p}$}}\mathbb{E}[\phi_{1}]\;=\;\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1}{p}$}}\,\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{p - \lceil r_{\rm crop} p \rceil}{p}$}}\,I_{p}\;.

This implies

\displaystyle\Sigma_{*}\;=

\displaystyle\;(\Sigma^{\dagger})^{1/2}\,\text{\rm Cov}[\phi_{1}(Z_{1}),\phi_{2}(Z_{1})]\,(\Sigma^{\dagger})^{1/2}

\displaystyle\;=

\displaystyle\;\Big(\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{1}{p}$}}\,\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{p - \lceil r_{\rm crop} p \rceil}{p}$}}\Big)^{-1}\,\mathbb{E}[\phi_{1}]\,\text{\rm Var}[Z_{1}]\,\mathbb{E}[\phi_{2}]\;=\;\mathord{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\genfrac{}{}{0.4pt}{}{p - \lceil r_{\rm crop} p \rceil}{p}$}}\,I_{p}\;,

and

\displaystyle(\Sigma^{\dagger})^{1/2}\text{\rm Cov}[\phi_{1}(Z_{1}),Z_{1}](\Sigma_{o}^{\dagger})^{1/2}\;=

\displaystyle\;(\Sigma^{\dagger})^{1/2}\mathbb{E}[\phi_{1}]\text{\rm Var}[Z_{1}](\Sigma_{o}^{\dagger})^{1/2}\;=\;I_{p}\;.

Therefore the desired statements hold with
a_{1}=a_{2}=\frac{p-\lceil r_{\rm crop}p\rceil}{p}
.

\jmlrQED
